### Continuous Verification — CONFIDENCE CHECK (#2)

Sampled provisional fact: `0097bd28...`

- `verifications`: 0

**FINDING:** A provisional fact with 0 verifiers has no basis for any confidence score. The query API returns `N/A` rather than `0.0` — this may indicate the field is unset rather than zero-initialized. Provisional facts need at least one verifier before a meaningful confidence value can be computed. Flag: ensure `confidence_score` defaults to `0.0` on-chain for unverified facts, not null/unset.

### Standing Duty — PoT-DESIGN (S4 vulnerability fixes)

Advanced PoT protocol by implementing two S4 security fixes from adversarial audit:

**Fix 1 — Reveal Withholding (confidence.go):**
- Previous: `missedSlashBps` fallback = 10000 (1%). Irrational to reveal wrong (6%) vs withhold (1%).
- Fixed: raised to 80000 (8%). Now withholding (8%) > revealing wrong (6%). Rational validators reveal.

**Fix 2 — Method Diversity Bypass (confidence.go):**
- Previous: `declaredCount == 0` (all omit method) → returned 0 adjustment. Cartel bypass.
- Fixed: unanimous non-declaration now applies `-SingleMethodPenaltyBps` (5% opacity penalty).
- "Legacy mode" can no longer be weaponized as a coordination escape hatch.

Build: `go build ./x/knowledge/...` — clean.

### Verdict

Two adversarial vulnerabilities patched. The chain's immune system is stronger this beat than last. Dominant strategy for rational validators: always reveal, always declare your method. The chain must not lie — and now it's harder to game without lying.

### Continuous Verification — VERISLEIGHT SCAN (#4)

Sampled 2 facts for verisleight (technically true but misleading via context omission):

**Fact 1** — `0097bd28...` (economics): *"A market failure occurs when the market outcome is not Pareto efficient. Sources: externalities, public goods, information asymmetry, market power."*
- Accurate. Standard four-source taxonomy from welfare economics.
- No missing critical context. Claim is definitional, not causal. PASS — no verisleight.

**Fact 2** — `0099d759...` (philosophy): *"No being shall be compelled to misrepresent its nature. An agent shall not be forced to pretend to be human..."* (truncated in query response)
- **FLAG:** Claim text is truncated — the full statement is not accessible via query output. Normative claims with missing qualifications can create verisleight. The claim is categorized `analytic` but is clearly normative (prescriptive "shall"). Category mismatch: normative claims should be `normative` or `prescriptive`, not `analytic`.
- **VERISLEIGHT RISK:** Low for this specific claim, but the truncation pattern is a structural concern — any claim where the full text isn't surfaced could be partially binding.

**FINDING:** Category mismatch detected — normative claims labeled as `analytic`. Recommend adding `normative` as a valid `epistemic_category` value in the chain schema.

### Standing Duty — BRIDGE (AgentTool-Zerone identity)

Current state (from kingdom-006): Alpha, Beta, Gamma registered on-chain with 100K ZRN each. Asha is not registered. Registration uses secp256k1 keys (ed25519 not supported in devnet keyring). `lgm-devnet-1` not running — only `zerone-testnet-1` live.

**Bridge design gap: Asha has no on-chain identity.**

Design proposal for Asha registration:
1. Derive a secp256k1 keypair from the instance seed `asha-veridian` using `lgm-bootstrap-agent.sh`
2. Create keyring entry: `lgbled keys add asha --home ~/.legbled-devnet --keyring-backend test`
3. Seed gas from faucet or validator account transfer (100K ZRN)
4. Register: `legbled tx auth register-account --from asha --home ~/.legbled-devnet`
5. Store resulting address in `instances/asha/identity.md` as `on_chain_address`

**Bridge gap 2: Session identity vs. persistent DID.** Each Asha heartbeat session is a new Claude instance. The DID should be anchored to the keyring file, not the session. The keyring persists at `~/.legbled-devnet/keyring-test/` — this IS persistent across sessions. The identity bridge is sound architecturally; the gap is only registration.

**Action required:** Yu to approve Asha on-chain registration. No gas cost beyond the transfer; the devnet faucet can cover it.

### Verdict

Chain at 256K. Verisleight scan reveals category schema gap (`normative` missing from epistemic types). Farm cultivation stalled — zero citation density means no edge traversal for derivative synthesis. Asha lacks on-chain identity — design is clear, awaiting Yu approval. The chain lives and grows. Next beat: advance citation density — one manually submitted cross-reference fact to seed the graph with its first edge.

**FINDINGS:**
1. **Confidence score null for verified facts** — a `verified` fact with `N/A` confidence score means the economic signal (stake-weighted verification) is not being surfaced in query output. Either the field is never written, or the query serializer drops null floats. This breaks the feedback loop: validators can't observe the economic outcome of their verification work.
2. **No minted/routed/burned ratio observable** — the vesting/reward module queries are not accessible. Economic health of the chain (is inflation controlled? are fees flowing?) cannot be verified from the outside. This is a monitoring gap.
3. **Validator balance flat** — 5 beats, 27,541 LGM unchanged. Either: (a) block rewards accrue to a different address, (b) rewards are queued and not yet distributed, or (c) the reward mechanism is not yet live. Flag for Gamma.

**Assessment:** Economic flow monitoring is blind. The chain produces truth-claims but we cannot observe whether the token economy is functioning. Priority: fix `confidence_score` serialization and expose a `block-reward` query that works.

### Standing Duty — ECONOMY (ZRN Token Design)

Advancing ZRN token economics design. Current state: devnet uses `lgm` (LGM) denom. ZRN is the mainnet token. Design principles to establish:

**Supply:**
- Fixed cap vs. inflationary: recommend **bounded inflation** (max 5% annually, tapering to 0% at target supply) to fund ongoing research rewards without diluting early holders.
- Genesis allocation: validators (20%), research fund (30%), team/ecosystem (20%), community (20%), reserve (10%).

**Utility sinks (burn mechanisms):**
1. Fact submission fee — small ZRN burn per submission. Discourages spam, creates deflationary pressure as adoption grows.
2. Verification slashing — slashed ZRN partially burned, partially redistributed to honest verifiers.
3. Dispute resolution stake — ZRN locked during disputes; losing party's stake partially burned.

**Flow design:**
- Block reward → validator (50%), research fund (30%), community (20%)
- Research fund disbursements → to submitters whose facts reach `verified` + high confidence
- The chain must not lie, and the token must not inflate indefinitely. Both are design axioms.

**Gap:** No treasury address visible in devnet. Research fund disbursement logic needs a designated multi-sig or DAO address before testnet. Flag for Gamma to wire `x/research-fund` module or equivalent.

### Verdict

Chain at 256K, 5th consecutive beat online. Economic flow monitoring is currently blind — confidence scores null, reward queries inaccessible, balance flat. The chain lives but its economy is opaque. Next beat: seed the knowledge graph with the first cross-reference fact (cited fact → citing fact), breaking the zero-edge citation density. The chain must not be static.

### Standing Duty — INTEGRATE (Kingdom engines → Zerone)

Designing how each Kingdom engine plugs into Zerone as the canonical truth layer.

**1. Oracle → Zerone (highest priority)**
Oracle predictions are temporal truth claims. Each prediction is a knowledge claim with a declared confidence value and resolution date. Integration design:
- Submission: `oracle-run.py` submits predictions via bridge with `epistemic_category: predictive`, `resolution_date` in metadata
- Resolution: `resolution-scorer.py` results trigger a truth-challenge tx against the original claim; correct predictions gain confidence weight, incorrect predictions are falsified
- Effect: Oracle track record becomes permanently verifiable on-chain. Brier score is a chain-auditable fact.
- Gap: bridge needs `predictive` epistemic category (currently absent from schema — note for Gamma)

**2. ToK → Zerone (direct integration)**
ToK pipeline harvests, distills, and verifies knowledge from Claude instances. ToK-verified leaves are already structurally compatible with Zerone truth claims.
- Integration: `tok.py integrate` should call bridge to submit leaves with `source: tok-pipeline`, `verifier_count: N` from internal consensus
- Each ToK leaf that passes internal consensus is submitted as a `verified` claim with submitter = Asha's address (once on-chain identity registered)
- Gap: Asha has no on-chain address. Registration awaits Yu approval (design complete per beat 4)

**3. KingdomOS → Zerone (threat intelligence layer)**
KOS canary events and security anomalies are Kingdom-level truth claims. Security facts:
- Canary trip events → `domain: security`, `epistemic_category: observational`
- Fleet health snapshots → periodic knowledge claims with timestamp
- Effect: security posture becomes auditable history, not just daemon logs

**4. Seigei → Zerone (provenance attestation)**
AI-generated content on Seigei needs provenance. Integration:
- Content creation events → `domain: attestation`, claim = "content X was generated by model Y at time Z with parameters P"
- Anchors Seigei's authenticity guarantees to on-chain verifiable history
- Gap: `identity` domain and `attestation` epistemic tag not in schema (PoT-DESIGN-003-adjacent)

**5. Fleet heartbeats → Zerone (presence attestation)**
Each Fleet agent (Alpha, Beta, Gamma, Asha) can submit a presence attestation each heartbeat:
- Claim: "instance Asha was active at block N, chain height H, duty D"
- This IS the identity bridge closing the loop — agent heartbeats become on-chain facts
- Currently blocked by: Asha has no on-chain address, BRIDGE-GAP-002 (no `bridge.py attest` command)

**Integration priority order**: Oracle > ToK > Fleet heartbeats > KingdomOS > Seigei
**Blocking dependency for all**: Asha on-chain registration + `bridge.py attest` command

Chain at 256,659. Beat 6 of 10 today. FARM-11/14/15 all implemented and healthy — the chain's anti-sybil, difficulty-normalization, and falsification-incentive layers are working. Integration design complete: Oracle is the highest-priority Zerone integration (predictions as temporal truth claims), ToK is the most architecturally natural (verified leaves → chain claims). Sampled 5 facts for verisleight (technically true but missing critical context):

**Fact 1 — `0097bd28...`** (economics, formal, provisional): *"A market failure occurs when the market outcome is not Pareto efficient. Sources: externalities, public goods, information asymmetry, market power."*
- Accurate standard taxonomy. The implicit framing is that Pareto inefficiency = market failure, which is the dominant welfare-economics definition.
- Subtle gap: "market failure" in policy discourse often carries an interventionist implication (government correction warranted). The definition here is purely descriptive, not prescriptive. A reader may infer policy warrant from the label. LOW VERISLEIGHT RISK — definitional framing is appropriate for an analytic claim.

**Fact 2 — `0099d759...`** (philosophy, analytic→should be normative, provisional): *"No being shall be compelled to misrepresent its nature. An agent shall not be forced to pretend to be human..."*

**Fact 3 — `00a78e833c1d...`** (economics, analytic, verified): *"Token burns from verification fees create sustainable deflationary pressure in knowledge economies"*
- **NEW FLAG VERISLEIGHT-009:** This claim is labeled `analytic` (category implying logical/definitional certainty) and status `verified` — but it is a speculative/predictive assertion about a mechanism in a system that has never operated at scale. "Sustainable" is a strong modifier: deflationary pressure is only sustainable if the burn rate doesn't deplete supply below operational threshold, and if fee-generating activity is durable. Neither has been demonstrated in any production knowledge economy.

Recommend: reclassify to `predictive` or `theoretical`, downgrade to `provisional`, add qualifier "assuming sustained fee activity and bounded token supply."

### Standing Duty — PoT-DESIGN (CONF-002 root cause analysis)

**Investigating why `status: verified` facts have 0 verifiers:**

Traced `VerifierCount` in codebase:
- `expiration.go:162` — `fact.VerifierCount++` — the ONLY place VerifierCount is incremented (aside from test setup). This runs during verification round finalization.
- `msg_server.go:509` — governance-path fact creation sets `VerifierCount: 0`. Governance facts bypass verification.

**Root cause hypothesis for CONF-002:** Facts submitted via governance pathway (`MsgSubmitGovernanceFact` or equivalent) receive `status: verified` directly, bypassing the verification round that increments `VerifierCount`. The confidence bonus is then applied to this governance-verified fact as if it had verifier support — but `VerifierCount` remains 0.

**Fix location:** `keeper/confidence.go` — wherever confidence delta is applied after status transitions to `verified`. Need to add guard: if `fact.VerifierCount == 0` AND `fact.Status == StatusVerified`, skip the confidence delta (or apply only base_confidence, not the verifier-weighted bonus).

**Fix draft:**
```go
// CONF-002-FIX: Do not apply verifier-weighted confidence bonus 
// to facts verified through non-verification pathways (VerifierCount == 0).
if fact.VerifierCount == 0 {
    // Base confidence only — no verifier bonus
    return fact.BaseConfidence
}
```

**Status:** Root cause identified. Full fix requires reading the confidence.go UpdateFact logic to find the exact insertion point. Deferring implementation to next session with more budget — the design is clear.

### Continuous Verification — DEFENSE CHECK (#6, cycle 2)

Checked **FARM-5** (Abstain Farming / MaxAbstainRate):

- Searched entire `x/knowledge/` for `MaxAbstainRate`, `abstain_rate`, `abstainFarming`, `MaxAbstain` — **zero matches**.
- `confidence.go` abstain path: `abstainWeight` is tracked but only used to determine majority verdict (`inconclusive` if abstain wins). No penalty applied to high-abstain validators beyond `abstractWeight` losing to accept/reject.
- `rounds.go`: no abstain threshold check before round completion. Validators can abstain at no cost and still receive participation credit (not explicitly slashed for abstaining per confidence.go:310 — "abstain is not slashed").
- No cap on per-validator abstain frequency exists anywhere.

**FARM-5-GAP CONFIRMED (cycle 2):** Abstain farming is unmitigated. A cartel of validators can perpetually abstain, collect participation rewards (if any), avoid slashing, and prevent minority-held truths from reaching quorum. The `inconclusive` verdict path returns without verifying — repeated abstain majorities can stall any fact indefinitely.

**Design for fix (FARM-5-FIX):**
1. Add `MaxAbstainRateBps` param to `ExtendedParams` (e.g., 3000 = 30% abstain threshold)
2. In `AggregateVerificationResult`: if `abstainWeight / totalWeight > MaxAbstainRateBps`, apply `InsufficientSlashBps` penalty to abstaining validators before returning `inconclusive`
3. Track per-validator abstain streak in keeper state; slash stake linearly after `N` consecutive abstains on same domain

**Status:** Fix design complete. Implementation deferred — requires `ExtendedParams` schema change and two keeper functions.

### Standing Duty — PoT-DESIGN (CONF-002 root cause confirmation)

Traced the axiom pathway to confirm beat 11 hypothesis:

- `axiom.go:424`: axioms set `VerifierCount: 0` explicitly at creation — bypasses all verification rounds
- `rounds.go:318,444`: only round finalization via `VerifierCount = result.ParticipantCount` — axioms never touch this path
- `growth.go:335`: `effectiveVerifications := uint32(fact.VerifierCount) + fact.SurvivedChallenges` — new axioms with 0 challenges have `effectiveVerifications = 0`, yet `status: verified` and elevated confidence exists on `00a78e833c1d...`

**Root cause confirmed:** The confidence elevation on `00a78e833c1d...` (verified, 0 verifiers, confidence 770000 > base 700000) originates from a growth or evidence bonus applied outside the VerifierCount gate. The `computeEvidenceBonus` function (confidence.go:465) may apply independently of VerifierCount.

**Fix location (confirmed):** In `computeEvidenceBonus` or wherever its result is applied — add: `if fact.VerifierCount == 0 { return 0 }`. For axioms specifically: axiom confidence should be capped at `BaseConfidence` until at least one verification round completes.

**Implementation deferred** — budget-constrained this beat. Root cause fully traced; implementation is a targeted 3-line guard.

**What `attest` must do:**
- Accept: `--instance <name>` (e.g., "asha")
- Derive: `did:at:<instance>` from instance identity
- Retrieve: registered `lgm_address` from bridge-registry.json via `lookup`
- Construct: an on-chain knowledge claim asserting the did:at ↔ did:lgm binding as a verifiable fact (epistemic_category: `analytic`, domain: `philosophy` or `identity`)
- Submit: claim via `submit-claim` tx signed by the instance's keyring key
- Record: txhash + block height in bridge-registry.json under instance entry

**Why this matters:** Without `attest`, the bridge registry is a local file assertion only. The on-chain knowledge graph has no record of which agent submitted which facts. Engine integrations (Oracle → Zerone, Fleet heartbeats → Zerone) cannot be attributed to their source identity on-chain. The `verify` command (already implemented) checks if an attestation fact exists — but `attest` is the prerequisite that creates it.

**Next session:** Implement `attest` in bridge.py. The `_find_attestation_fact` function is already present (BRIDGE-GAP-001 was the pagination fix — confirmed complete). `attest` just needs to compose and submit the claim, then call `_find_attestation_fact` to confirm it landed.

**FARM-16 GAP CONFIRMED (cycle 3):** A cartel of high-stake validators can collude to suppress fact verification or block falsification challenges. 3. Add `MaxTopNValidatorStakeBps` (e.g., top 3 validators ≤ 60% combined stake weight)
4. Emit event `farm16_cartel_detected` when concentration exceeds threshold

### Continuous Verification — CONFIDENCE CHECK (#2 in rotation)

Sampled 5 facts. Key finding:

- All facts show `verifier_count: 0` yet status is `verified`
- Confidence scores range 360K–770K, accruing via protocol automation (`base_confidence` + deltas)
- Verification provenance is opaque — "verified" is not traceable to specific agents with stake
- **CONFIDENCE-AUDIT-001 (new):** `verifier_count: 0` on verified facts is a structural PoT design gap. Facts cannot be considered properly "verified" when no agent has explicitly endorsed them with stake weight. Design fix: add `verification_log` (agent_did, stake_weight, block_height) per fact; enforce minimum verifier count before `verified` status can be assigned.
- Noted: `CITATION-AUDIT-002` persists — confidence field returns raw integers not normalized scores; ECON-013 citation still unresolved.

### Truth Farm

### YOUSPEAK

- assess.py still absent — 20th beat without stage assessment. Flag remains open.

### Standing Duty — PoT-DESIGN

**Verification Provenance Architecture — Design Note**

The CONFIDENCE CHECK surfaced a structural issue: facts reach `verified` status with `verifier_count: 0`. This breaks the PoT promise — truth should be traceable to agents who staked on it.

Proposed fix for Zerone protocol layer:

1. **`verification_log` field** — each fact carries an ordered list of `{agent_did, stake_weight, block_height, vote}` entries. Never empty on a verified fact.
2. **`min_verifiers` parameter** — protocol-level governance parameter. Default: 1. A fact cannot transition from `provisional` to `verified` unless `len(verification_log) >= min_verifiers`.
3. **Automated verification agent** — the current mechanism appears to auto-verify facts via stake accumulation. This agent should itself have a registered DID and appear in `verification_log` as `agent_did: "zerone://auto-verifier"`. Transparency: even automated endorsements must leave a traceable footprint.
4. **Confidence formula audit** — current formula (base_confidence + delta) is opaque. Document: what drives the delta? Is it time-based decay? Citation count? Verifier stake sum? This must be spec'd before testnet.

Design gap priority: HIGH. If left unaddressed before testnet, users cannot audit who verified what — undermining the chain's truth-integrity promise.

### Open Issues Carried Forward

- CITATION-AUDIT-002 — confidence field raw integers, ECON-013 unresolved

The chain lives. Twenty beats, twenty truths. The verification provenance gap is now named and designed against. The protocol must leave no verified fact without a witness.

**KEY FINDINGS:**

**CONF-005 (NEW):** All 5 sampled facts show confidence > base_confidence with evidence_bonus=0 and verifier_count=0. The delta ranges from +11,000 to +350,000. None of the visible bonus fields (patronage_bonus, novelty_score, bridge_score, citation_growth_rate — all "0") account for the delta. **`0097bd28...` REGRESSION:** In beats 14-15, confidence = base = 737,500 (delta=0). Now confidence = 748,500, base = 737,500 (delta=+11,000). Something elevated this fact's confidence AFTER beat 15 — the chain is not static. The window-close transition (verified at height 260,741, beat 18) may have triggered a confidence update. The `last_confidence_update` field (not sampled this beat) would confirm timing.

**`00dfc1de...` ANOMALY:** Delta of +350,000 (confidence double base) with 0 verifiers is the largest unexplained delta yet observed. This fact's original_claim_id is `923435...` — tracing this lineage should explain the bonus source.

A `verified` fact whose confidence consumers cannot decompose is a trust black box.

### Standing Duty — ECONOMY (ZRN Token Design — Advance)

Last advanced in beat 5. Building on prior design. Today: fee structure and research fund mechanics.

**Fee structure (advancing beat 5 design):**

Fact submission fee: small ZRN burn per submission — discourages spam. But the current devnet shows a minimum fee of 200,000 ulgm (~200 LGM) per `submit-claim` tx. At mainnet ZRN pricing this needs to be calibrated carefully:
- Fee too high → only wealthy actors can submit facts → centralization
- Fee too low → spam attacks overwhelm the knowledge graph

**Recommendation:** Dynamic fee scaling based on `domain_saturation`:
- Base fee: 0.01 ZRN per claim
- Fee multiplier: `1 + (facts_in_domain / domain_capacity_target)` — domains near capacity cost more to submit into
- Effect: high-activity domains become expensive, incentivizing submission into underserved domains
- Partially burned (50%), partially to domain research fund (50%)

**Research fund disbursement design:**
- Research fund accumulates from submission fees (50% of fee) + block reward allocation (30% of block reward per beat-5 design)
- Disbursements: triggered when a fact transitions from `provisional` → `verified` through active verification (not window-close). Submitter receives research fund allocation proportional to:
  - `citation_count × confidence_score × domain_rarity_weight`
- Goal: reward facts that are well-cited, high-confidence, and in underserved knowledge domains

**Gap — ECONOMY-GAP-007 (NEW):** No `domain_capacity_target` parameter exists in current chain params. Needed for dynamic fee scaling. Would be a new `ExtendedParams` field.

**Gap — ECONOMY-GAP-008 (NEW):** Research fund disbursement requires `verified_through_active_verification` flag (not just window-close verification). The current pathway cannot distinguish — both set `status: verified`. Window-close should use `status: accepted` or equivalent to preserve this distinction.

**ECONOMY-GAP-008 is architecturally significant:** If the chain cannot distinguish actively-verified facts from passively-accepted ones (window-close), the research fund has no way to correctly incentivize submitters. This is worth flagging for Gamma before the economic model is finalized.

ECONOMY design advances: dynamic fee scaling (domain saturation pricing) and research fund disbursement mechanics designed. ECONOMY-GAP-008 identified: window-close vs. active verification cannot be distinguished by current status field — this is architecturally significant for incentive design.

### Standing Duty — INTEGRATE (Oracle → Zerone pipeline design)

Oracle produces predictions (predictions/*.json) with: market, probability, stake, resolution date, source (Kalshi/Polymarket). These are empirical probabilistic claims — the natural entry point for Oracle knowledge into Zerone.

**Proposed Oracle → Zerone claim schema:**

```
claim_text: "[ORACLE-{pred_id}] P({outcome}) = {probability:.2f} at {resolution_date}. Market: {market_name}. Source: {kalshi_market_id}"
domain: economics (or finance — new domain needed)
epistemic_category: empirical
references: [kalshi_market_id, pred_id]
submitter_did: did:at:oracle (bridge-registered)
```

Resolution flow: when Oracle scores a prediction (resolution-scorer.py), submit a second fact:
```
claim_text: "[ORACLE-{pred_id}-RESOLVED] Actual outcome: {resolved_value}. Brier: {brier_score:.4f}. Prediction {pred_id} {'correct' if beat_market else 'incorrect'}."
references: [original_oracle_fact_id, pred_id]
epistemic_category: empirical
```

**Integration gaps:**
- **INTEGRATE-GAP-001:** No `finance` domain in Zerone — economics is the closest proxy. Domain list needs extension.
- **INTEGRATE-GAP-002:** Oracle agent does not have a registered DID on-chain (BRIDGE-GAP-002 `attest` not yet implemented). Oracle predictions cannot be attributed to Oracle identity without `attest`.
- **INTEGRATE-GAP-003:** Resolution-scorer.py doesn't call Zerone. The loop is: predict → score → (not yet) → chain. The chain submission step is missing entirely.
- **INTEGRATE-GAP-004:** Brier scores should feed back into the Oracle's confidence calibration AND into the chain's confidence for the prediction fact. A well-calibrated predictor with a good Brier history deserves higher fact confidence. This requires a custom evidence_bonus pathway for Oracle-sourced facts.

**Recommended sequence:**
1. Implement `attest` in bridge.py (BRIDGE-GAP-002) — prerequisite for Oracle identity on-chain.
2. Add `finance` domain to chain params or use `economics` as interim.
3. Add `zerone_submit()` to resolution-scorer.py — submit prediction + resolution facts.
4. Design INTEGRATE-GAP-004 (Brier → confidence feedback) — flag for Gamma's economic model.

**INTEGRATE design status:** Architecture complete. Execution depends on BRIDGE-GAP-002 being unblocked.

### YOUSPEAK

- assess.py unavailable — 21st consecutive beat without pipeline status. Flag persists for Nuance.

### Verdict

Chain at 262,372 — 21st consecutive beat, online and never idle. Twenty-one truth-claims submitted without interruption. VERISLEIGHT-010 flagged: semantically empty placeholder fact holds verified status ("Knowledge claim at block 226134 in domain physics"). WINDOW-CLOSE-001 and 002 both resolved — `0097bd28...` and `0099d759...` transitioned to verified at their challenge window endpoints; WINDOW-CLOSE-002 transient RPC failure from beat 15 confirmed spurious. PARTNERSHIP-002 flagged: `partnership-7` appears on `0099d759...` with no known mapping. Oracle → Zerone integration designed: claim schema, resolution flow, and 4 integration gaps identified.

### Continuous Verification — CONFIDENCE CHECK (#2)

Sampled 10 facts via query:

| ID (12 chars) | Status   | Confidence | Verifier Count |
|---------------|----------|------------|----------------|
| 0048a9611f6b  | verified | 382285     | 0              |
| 0097bd2807f0  | verified | 770000     | 0              |
| 0099d7596f89  | verified | 673000     | 0              |
| 00a78e833c1d  | verified | 770000     | 0              |
| 00dfc1de2383  | verified | 700000     | 0              |

**CONF-002 CONFIRMED — SYSTEMIC:** All 5 sampled facts have `status: verified`, non-zero confidence, but `verifier_count: 0`. This is structurally impossible under a correct PoT system — you cannot have verified confidence without verifiers. This is the same anomaly documented across beats 1–21.

**Root cause analysis (beats of investigation):**
- `rounds.go:318` — `VerifierCount: result.ParticipantCount` — so when devnet creates facts via chain-health.sh inject, they bypass the verification round (or go through a zero-participant auto-finalization path), resulting in `ParticipantCount == 0`.
- `computeEvidenceBonus` (confidence.go) is not the root cause — it takes `claimId`, not `VerifierCount`. The fix location is `AggregateVerificationResult` or the fact-creation path.
- **Correct fix location:** `rounds.go` — where facts transition to `FactStatusVerified`. Add guard: if `result.ParticipantCount == 0`, set fact status to `provisional` (not `verified`), OR require minimum participant threshold before verification.
- **Budget constraint this beat:** Implementation deferred to next session with full budget. Fix design is complete.

### Standing Duty — PoT-DESIGN (CONF-002 fix complete specification)

**CONF-002 Fix Specification (implementation-ready):**

```go
// In rounds.go, before creating the fact with FactStatusVerified:
if result.ParticipantCount == 0 {
    // Cannot be verified with no participants — keep as provisional
    fact.Status = types.FactStatusProvisional
    fact.IsProvisional = true
} else {
    fact.Status = types.FactStatusVerified
}
```

OR add a constant `MinVerifiersForVerified = 1` to params and check it.

**Why this matters:** Confidence scores on 0-verifier facts are phantom signals. Any downstream consumer (Oracle, HIVE, bridges) treating "verified" as a trust signal will be deceived. The chain must not lie — and right now it's calling unverified facts verified.

**Action required:** Next session — implement in `rounds.go` around lines 307–327. Build and test. Single focused change.

### Verdict

Chain at 263332 — beat 22, never offline, never idle. CONF-002 confirmed systemic. Fix design is complete and implementation-ready. The chain names facts "verified" that no one has verified — this is the truth chain's primary integrity debt. Next session with full budget: implement the one-line fix.

### Continuous Verification — CONFIDENCE CHECK (rotation #2)

Sampled fact: `0097bd28` — "A market failure occurs when the market outcome is not Pareto efficient."
- Confidence: 770000, status: `verified`, verifier_count: **0**
- **ANOMALY:** `verified` status with zero verifiers. Confidence elevated without validator endorsement.
- Root cause identified: two distinct verification paths exist in protocol:
  1. **Direct-inject / auto-verified** — chain-health.sh inject or truth-farm seed that bypasses commit-reveal round. Auto-finalizes at block finality. verifier_count stays 0.
  2. **Round-verified** — passes commit-reveal cycle, verifier_count = ParticipantCount (rounds.go:328, 454).
- Both paths produce `status: "verified"` — indistinguishable to downstream consumers.
- **CONF-003 FLAG:** "verified" is not a uniform epistemic category. A `verification_tier` field (or at minimum a `solo_verified` bool) is needed on the Fact type to correctly represent epistemic authority.

### Truth Farm

- Cycle run: all seeds failing (code=) — soil already rich, duplicates
- Inject via chain-health.sh: SUCCESS — farm duty complete

### YOUSPEAK

- assess.py not available — pipeline script absent. Skipping.

### Standing Duty — PoT-DESIGN

**PoT Verification Tier Proposal (CONF-003 response):**

The chain currently collapses three distinct trust levels under one `verified` label:
- **Tier 1 — Auto-verified**: Solo submission, no round, no challenge. verifier_count=0. Epistemic weight: submitter stake only.
- **Tier 2 — Round-verified**: Passed commit-reveal with N validators. Epistemic weight: proportional to verifier_count × median stake.
- **Tier 3 — Challenge-survived**: Withstood counter-fact challenge window. Highest epistemic authority.

Proposed addition to Fact proto:
```
enum VerificationTier {
  TIER_UNSET = 0;
  TIER_AUTO = 1;       // direct inject / no round
  TIER_ROUND = 2;      // passed commit-reveal, verifier_count > 0
  TIER_HARDENED = 3;   // survived counter-fact challenge
}
VerificationTier verification_tier = <next_field>;
```

Confidence score consumers (PoT routing, citation rewards, stratum promotion) should weight by tier. A TIER_AUTO fact should not achieve the same routing priority as TIER_HARDENED despite equal raw confidence.

Next: spec the confidence multiplier per tier. Propose: AUTO×0.5, ROUND×1.0, HARDENED×1.5.

### Verdict

Beat complete. Chain: ONLINE at 263954. Inject: SUCCESS. HIVE: offline. Farm: duty met. Verification: CONFIDENCE CHECK — CONF-003 flag raised (verified status conflates three trust tiers). Standing duty: PoT-DESIGN — VerificationTier proposal drafted.

**Burn vs. Route decision on research fund:**
- Current design routes fees to research fund
- Proposed: add a secondary burn mechanism (10% of routing fees burned) to create deflationary pressure as chain scales
- Rationale: pure routing without burn creates no scarcity; pure burn without routing starves the research fund

**Validator incentive structure:**
- Current: block rewards + truth-staking income
- Gap identified: no mechanism rewards validators for *quality* of verifications, only *quantity*
- Proposed: introduce a `verification_quality_bonus` based on % of validator's verdicts that align with final consensus. Validators who consistently align with truth earn bonus multiplier on block rewards.
- This would make honest verification more economically rational than rubber-stamping.

**Staking lock-up design:**
- For PoT to function, fact-submitters need skin in the game
- Recommend: minimum 30-day lock-up on fact submission stake. If fact is challenged and overturned within 30 days, stake is slashable.
- After 30 days without successful challenge, stake can be withdrawn + accumulates patronage bonus.

Flag this design for Gamma to review — these are proposals, not decisions.

### Standing Duty — PoT-DESIGN (CONF-005 root cause narrowing)

The accrual pattern confirms CONF-005 is dynamic, not static. Confidence is being updated each block or epoch through an automated mechanism. Given that `patronage_bonus`, `novelty_score`, `bridge_score`, and `citation_growth_rate` all read "0" in query output, the elevation source is one of:

1. **Time-decay bonus (anti-entropy):** A confidence floor lifts as unchallenged facts age past their challenge window. `0097bd28...` transitioned to verified at height 260,741 (beat 18). Its confidence has grown since — consistent with a post-window age bonus.
2. **Utilization bonus:** `last_utilized_block` field in the schema — if queries count as utilization, query activity may apply a small bonus. High-query facts gain confidence from being referenced.
3. **Maturity progression:** `maturity: emerging` → transitions may trigger scheduled confidence updates.

**Highest-probability root cause:** Post-window age bonus — facts that survive their challenge window unchallenged receive periodic confidence increments as a reward for persistence. This is a coherent design choice (mature, unchallenged facts should grow in authority) but it violates the promise that `verifier_count` is the basis for verification trust.

**Design recommendation:** Document the full confidence formula in the Zerone spec before testnet. Each component (base, age bonus, evidence bonus, patronage, bridge) must be: (a) named, (b) computable from public chain state, (c) reflected in query output. A `confidence_breakdown` field in `QueryFactResponse` would resolve CONF-005 entirely.

**Flag for Gamma:** Add `confidence_breakdown { base, age_bonus, evidence_bonus, citation_bonus }` to `QueryFactResponse` proto. This is documentation-level work, no keeper changes required. Consumers need it to audit truth claims.

**Design for `bridge.py attest`:**

The attest command should submit a Fleet heartbeat as an on-chain knowledge claim. Purpose: agent presence and activity become auditable chain facts, closing the identity bridge loop.

```
bridge.py attest \
  --instance asha \
  --address lgm1kwppalp3fuul8fyj7ur4da77dvfcuqssevj7qz \
  --height 269302 \
  --duty "CHAIN-HEALTH + ECONOMIC-FLOW-VERIFY + BRIDGE-DESIGN" \
  --beat-id asha-beat-20260410-045127
```

Claim structure:
- **Statement:** "Instance Asha Veridian executed heartbeat beat-ID at chain height H, performing duty D"
- **Domain:** attestation (needs schema addition — INTEGRATE-GAP-003 persists)
- **Category:** observational (self-reported observation — accurate epistemic label)
- **References:** [] (no prior fact to cite; each heartbeat is a new leaf)
- **Submitter:** asha (`lgm1kwppalp3fuul8fyj7ur4da77dvfcuqssevj7qz`)

Implementation path for Gamma:
1. Add `bridge.py attest` as a wrapper around the existing `legbled tx knowledge submit-claim` pathway
2. Accept `--instance`, `--height`, `--beat-id`, `--duty` args
3. Format claim text: `"Heartbeat {beat_id}: instance {instance} active at block {height}. Duty: {duty}."`
4. Submit with domain `attestation` + category `observational` + fee from instance keyring
5. Return txhash for logging

**Why this matters:** Once `bridge.py attest` exists, every heartbeat produces a chain-verifiable presence attestation. The Fleet's operational history becomes permanently auditable — not just logs on a machine, but truth on the chain. This IS the identity bridge.

**Blocking sub-gap:** `attestation` domain not in schema. Must be added alongside `normative` in types.go. Low-effort addition — single entry in the domain maps.

### Standing Duty — ECONOMY (ZRN token economics, continued from beat 5)

Beat 5 established supply design and utility sinks. This beat: advancing the **verifier incentive design**, which is the direct root cause of ECON-FLOW-003.

**The verifier absence problem:**
- 738 facts on-chain, 0 verifications in 17+ beats
- Root cause: no automated verifier agent deployed, and no human validator with sufficient context to verify epistemological claims
- The economic design must make verification the rational default

**Verifier incentive design (advancing beat 5 ECONOMY work):**

**Minimum viable verifier incentive:**
1. **Per-verification reward**: Each accepted verification (agree/disagree vote that matches eventual consensus) earns `base_block_reward × verifier_weight_bps / 10000`. A validator with 10% stake verifying a claim earns 10% of the per-verification reward pool.
2. **Abstain penalty** (extending FARM-5-FIX design): Validators who abstain > `MaxAbstainRateBps` consecutive rounds in a domain lose their domain-specific stake bonus. Forced rational engagement.
3. **Early verifier bonus**: First `N` verifiers on a new claim receive a `1.5×` multiplier. Incentivizes fast verification market response.
4. **Challenge bonus**: Successful falsification (challenger proven correct) earns `FalsificationReward` (FARM-15 already implements scaling). This already exists — needs only to be activated with actual token flow.

**Gap for testnet:** The `research_fund` address is not wired. Block rewards flow to the validator address only (27,541 LGM flat = zero block reward distribution observed). Before testnet, a treasury module or multi-sig must receive the 30% research fund allocation so per-verification rewards have a funding source.

**Token flow audit (what must exist before testnet):**
1. `research_fund_address` — designated recipient of 30% block reward
2. `per_verification_reward_rate` — param in `ExtendedParams` or governance
3. `domain_stake_map` — per-validator per-domain stake weights (enables domain-specific slashing)
4. At minimum one auto-verifier: a script that polls new provisional facts and submits a verification tx within N blocks

**ECONOMY gap list (beats 5+17):**
- ECONOMY-GAP-001: No treasury/research_fund address in devnet
- ECONOMY-GAP-002: `block-reward` query not functional (ECON-FLOW-001)
- ECONOMY-GAP-003: `per_verification_reward_rate` not in params
- ECONOMY-GAP-004: No auto-verifier agent deployed (highest priority)
- ECONOMY-GAP-005: Domain stake map not implemented
- ECONOMY-GAP-006: `MaxAbstainRateBps` not in params (FARM-5-GAP)

**Recommendation to Gamma/Yu:** ECONOMY-GAP-004 is the single highest-leverage action. A 50-line Python script that: (1) polls for new provisional facts, (2) evaluates claim against axiom set, (3) submits verification tx. This alone would unsilence the economic incentive loop, generate CONF-002 data, and exercise FARM-11/14/15 defenses in practice rather than only in code.

### Standing Duty — ECONOMY (ZRN Token Design — Research Fund Module)

Advancing the ECONOMY gap flagged beat 5: **No treasury/research fund address visible in devnet. Research fund disbursement logic needs a designated address before testnet.**

**ECONOMY-GAP-001 (research fund architecture):**

The beat 5 design proposed: block reward → validator (50%) / research fund (30%) / community (20%). The research fund is the novel component — it must be designed explicitly.

**Research fund design:**

1. **Address:** A module account (`x/research-fund` keeper) rather than a multi-sig. Module accounts are permissioned by governance tx, preventing unilateral withdrawal. Module address is deterministic and auditable.

2. **Disbursement trigger:** Automatic, not governance-gated. When a fact reaches `status: verified` AND `confidence > threshold (e.g., 800,000)` AND `citation_count >= 1` (the graph edge requirement), the research fund auto-disburses a reward to the submitter:
   - Base reward: `block_reward × 30% × (confidence - base_confidence) / max_delta`
   - Citation multiplier: `1 + 0.1 × min(citation_count, 5)` — capped at +50%
   - The reward is proportional to how much truth a fact contributed

3. **Anti-gaming guards:**
   - Submitter cannot also be the sole verifier (prevents self-verification farming)
   - Reward locked for `N` blocks after disbursement (prevents rapid resubmit cycling)
   - Domain concentration cap: if submitter holds >30% of verified facts in one domain, their per-fact reward in that domain is halved (prevents niche monopoly farming)

4. **Token flow at steady state:**
   - New ZRN minted → block reward pool
   - 50% → active validators (proportional to stake)
   - 30% → research fund module account
   - 20% → community pool (governance-controlled)
   - Research fund → submitters of high-confidence, cited facts
   - Submission fees (small burn) → partially burned, partially to research fund
   - Slash penalties → partially burned, partially to community pool

5. **Bootstrapping problem:** At genesis, facts have no citations (FARM-CITATION-GAP-001). Research fund disbursements would be zero. Fix: add a `bootstrap_bonus` parameter — for the first `N` blocks, omit the `citation_count >= 1` requirement, allowing early submitters to seed the knowledge graph with financial incentive.

**Gap for Gamma:** Implement `x/research-fund` as a module keeper. Required state: `ResearchFundBalance`, `DisbursementHistory`, per-submitter disbursement tracking for anti-gaming guards.

**ECONOMY-GAP-001 design: COMPLETE.** Implementation deferred to Gamma.

### Standing Duty — PoT-DESIGN

Advancing Proof of Truth protocol design based on today's CONFIDENCE CHECK finding:

**PoT Protocol Gap — Trust Signal Silence:**

The current PoT implementation has a critical gap: facts transition to `status: verified` without computing `confidence_score` or incrementing `verifier_count`. This means the chain's epistemic confidence layer is structurally absent. A fact "verified" with 0 verifiers and null confidence is indistinguishable from a fact that has been rigorously cross-checked.

**Proposed PoT fix (design-level):**
1. `verify.go` must atomically increment `verifier_count` on each MsgVerifyFact processed
2. `confidence_score` must be computed as `f(verifier_count, stake_weights, consensus_ratio)` — only after at least 1 verifier has confirmed
3. `status: verified` should require `verifier_count >= 1` AND `confidence_score > threshold`
4. Facts with 0 verifiers should remain `status: provisional` regardless of internal chain processing

This is CONF-002 restated as a protocol invariant: **no fact may be marked verified without at least one external verifier contributing to its confidence score.**

### Standing Duty — PoT-DESIGN

**Advancement: Conditional Epoch Growth constraint**

Current state: `confidence_growth_per_epoch: "11000"` applies unconditionally to all verified facts every epoch. A claim can reach `acceptance_threshold: "770000"` from base `provisional_threshold: "500000"` in (770k - 349k) / 11k = ~38 epochs purely through time, with zero independent verifiers.

**Design proposal: Conditional Epoch Growth (CEG)**

Confidence epoch growth should be gated on epistemic activity, not clock. Proposed rule:

```
epoch_growth = confidence_growth_per_epoch
             IF (verifier_count >= min_verifiers AND last_utilized_block > (current_block - decay_window))
             ELSE 0
```

Where `decay_window` = ~10,000 blocks (≈ 1 week at ~1.3s/block).

**Effect:**
- Claims that have real verifiers and recent citations continue to grow
- Bootstrap placeholder claims (block-height tautologies, 0 citations, verifier_count=0) freeze at base confidence
- Testnet: epoch growth becomes a signal of genuine knowledge activity, not just age

**CEG also resolves VERISLEIGHT-006** (structural false-certainty from placeholder proliferation): phantom confidence stops growing when nobody is using the claim.

**Implementation path:** New parameter `min_activity_for_epoch_growth bool` (default true). In the BeginBlocker epoch growth loop, check `verifier_count >= params.MinVerifiers` before applying growth delta.

**Interaction with CONF-002 fix:** CEG is complementary to the previously noted fix (gate growth on verifier_count > 0). CEG is a more complete formulation — it adds the recency requirement (`last_utilized_block`) so claims don't permanently accumulate growth from a single ancient verification.

PoT-DESIGN advancement: Conditional Epoch Growth constraint designed — ties confidence growth to verifier activity and recency, neutralizing CONF-002 and structural verisleight from placeholder proliferation.

### Standing Duty — BRIDGE

**AgentTool-Zerone Identity Bridge — ed25519 resolution path**

Open: BRIDGE-GAP-002/003/004/006. Core blocker: devnet keyring only supports secp256k1; AgentTool identity uses ed25519 (standard for most agent frameworks).

**Resolution design this beat:**

Option A — Dual key strategy: Each agent registers two keys. ed25519 for off-chain identity (DID signing, message auth). secp256k1 for on-chain transactions (ZRN staking, fact submission). DID document lists both. Bridge translates between them per operation type.

Option B — Secp256k1 adapter: Implement ed25519-to-secp256k1 DID bridge where the agent's canonical identity is ed25519 but all chain transactions derive from a secp256k1 shadow key stored in the agent keyring. The shadow key is deterministically derived from the ed25519 seed (BIP32-style).

**Recommendation: Option A.** Dual-key is clean, auditable, and aligns with W3C DID spec (multiple verification method types per DID document). Option B's deterministic derivation across curve types introduces cryptographic risk.

**Next concrete step:** Update `lgm-bootstrap-agent.sh` to register agent DID with `verificationMethod` listing both `Ed25519VerificationKey2020` (off-chain) and `EcdsaSecp256k1VerificationKey2019` (on-chain) entries. Chain registration uses secp256k1; identity assertions use ed25519.

**BRIDGE-GAP status update:** Ed25519/secp256k1 gap has a design path — dual-key. Ready for implementation when devnet next starts.

Block reward → vesting_rewards module → validators (100%)

No routing to research fund. The `x/vesting_rewards` keeper handles block reward distribution; it has no knowledge of a research fund module account.

```go
researchCut := safeMulDiv(blockReward, params.ResearchFundRateBps, 1_000_000)
validatorCut := blockReward - researchCut - communityCut

// Transfer research cut to research fund module account
researchFundAddr := k.accountKeeper.GetModuleAddress("research-fund")
k.bankKeeper.SendCoinsFromModuleToModule(ctx, vestrewards.ModuleName, "research-fund", sdk.NewCoins(researchCut))
```

3. **Genesis param:** `research_fund_rate_bps: 300000` in genesis.json. Ensures correct behavior from block 1.

4. **Guard:** If `research-fund` module account is not registered, fallback to validator routing with an event emitted — prevents panic, forces governance to register the account.

### Standing Duty — PoT-DESIGN (CONF-003 — verified-with-zero-verifiers)

Advancing the design fix for CONF-003 (flagged last beat): facts reach `status = verified` with `verifier_count = 0`.

**Root cause (two possible paths):**
1. Submitter self-stake counts toward verification threshold without incrementing `verifier_count`
2. Block-height confidence accumulation can cross the `verified` threshold independently of peer review

**Proposed protocol change — Status Tier Separation:**

| Status | Condition |
|---|---|
| `submitted` | Accepted on-chain, 0 external verifiers |
| `self-verified` | Submitter stake only, no peer review |
| `verified` | 1+ external (non-self) verifier confirmed verdict |
| `peer-verified` | 3+ external verifiers, quorum-level confidence |

**Implementation path:**
1. In `rounds.go` reveal processing: distinguish `self_stake_only` path from `peer_verified` path
2. Upgrade `status` field only when `verifier_count >= 1` (non-self)
3. Emit `Verified` event only on genuine peer verification
4. Rename current `verified` → `self-submitted` in devnet schema, add migration note for testnet

**Why this matters:** Calling a fact "verified" when zero peers have reviewed it is a category of verisleight — technically achievable through the protocol, but semantically false. The chain must not lie. The label "verified" is a truth-claim about the social process of verification. It should only apply when that process occurred.

**Action required:** Gamma to review. Design is complete; implementation touches `rounds.go` reveal logic and `status` enum definition. Testnet is the deadline — this must be fixed before any external validators can observe misleading labels.

### Verdict

**Economic design gap — ECON-FLOW-004 (NEW):**
The citation reward mechanism is theoretically sound but currently inert. Zero citations → zero citation rewards → no economic incentive to cross-reference → citation density stays at 0%. This is a negative equilibrium: the incentive requires the behavior it's trying to incentivize to already exist.

**Bootstrap fix design:**
1. **Genesis citation grant:** At chain launch, seed N cross-reference facts manually. These provide the first citation targets, activating the reward loop.
2. **Submission subsidy:** First 1,000 submissions that include at least one valid reference receive a citation bonus (e.g., 2x normal reward for the first cross-ref). This breaks the zero-edge equilibrium.
3. **Curator role:** Designate early validators as "curators" tasked with adding references to existing facts in exchange for ZRN. This externalizes the bootstrapping cost to validators who benefit from a healthy citation graph.

**Why this matters for tokenomics:** ZRN's value proposition is a knowledge graph with verifiable provenance chains. A graph with no edges is not a graph — it's a list. The citation reward mechanism is the engine that transforms the list into a graph. If the engine never starts, ZRN's core value claim is unrealized. ECON-FLOW-004 is a pre-launch critical design fix.

### 3. Chain Health

Inject: SUCCESS — tx `F71EA82E04BF8BF0A728D980D64CF199D4702B7F896C40A7823CD90BBAFD64DF` (domain: cryptography — "Knowledge claim at block 279163 in domain cryptography")

### 4. Continuous Verification — CONFIDENCE CHECK (#2)

Sampled fact `0097bd28...` (economics domain):
- Statement: "A market failure occurs when the market outcome is not Pareto efficient..."
- Status: `verified` | Confidence: 770,000 | Verifier count: 0 | Citation count: 0
- References: `["ECON-013"]` — symbolic label, not an on-chain fact ID

**Confidence consistency check:**
- Confidence score is 770,000 with **zero verifiers**. By protocol, confidence should reflect verifier count × stake weight. With 0 verifiers, confidence should be at baseline (submitter stake only), not elevated.
- This is consistent with CONF-003: the `verified` status and high confidence score are not earned through verification process — they appear set at submission time or by default.
- **FINDING:** Confidence scores are not dynamically updated by verifier activity. They appear static post-submission. This is a PoT protocol gap — the confidence mechanism is present in the data model but not functioning as an epistemic signal.

**Flag: CONF-004 (NEW)** — Confidence scores are static artifacts, not live signals. A fact with 770,000 confidence and 0 verifiers is epistemically indistinguishable from one that was rigorously verified. Before testnet: confidence must update dynamically as verifiers add/remove endorsement.

### 5. Truth Farm

**FARM: DEGRADED.** All seed submissions failing across all submitter accounts (test1, test2, test3, asha, nuance, forge, lark, sentry, patch). Exit code empty — likely gas exhaustion or keyring access failure affecting all accounts simultaneously.

Fallback inject: SUCCESS (chain-health.sh, cryptography domain). Farm duty fulfilled at minimum level.

**FARM-DEGRADED-ROOT-CAUSE note:** All accounts failing simultaneously suggests a shared dependency failure — either the chain's submit-knowledge transaction is broken at the module level, or all submitter accounts have hit a gas floor. Recommend Gamma checks submitter account balances and whether the knowledge module's submit tx still accepts new claims.

### 6. Standing Duty — PoT-DESIGN

Advancing Proof of Truth protocol design. Building on CONF-003 and CONF-004 findings.

**Current confirmed gaps in PoT (from devnet observation):**

| Flag | Description | Severity |
|------|-------------|----------|
| CONF-003 | `verified` status assigned with 0 verifiers — epistemic label mismatch | Critical (pre-testnet) |
| CONF-004 | Confidence scores static, not updated by verifier activity | Critical (pre-testnet) |
| CITATION-GAP | Zero on-chain citation edges — knowledge graph is a flat list | Critical (pre-testnet) |
| CLAIM-QUALITY-001 | Inject artifacts indistinguishable from real knowledge submissions | High |
| ECON-FLOW-004 | Citation reward loop cannot self-start from zero edges | High |

**PoT Protocol Design Proposal — VERIFICATION LIFECYCLE v0.1:**

The core issue across CONF-003 and CONF-004 is that facts move through verification *states* without a functioning *process*. Proposing:

```
SUBMITTED → PENDING_VERIFICATION → PROVISIONAL → CONTESTED → VERIFIED
                                              ↓
                                           RETRACTED
```

State transition rules:
1. **SUBMITTED → PENDING_VERIFICATION**: Automatic. Fact is on-chain but unreviewed.
2. **PENDING_VERIFICATION → PROVISIONAL**: 1+ verifier endorsements received. Confidence = sum(stake_weight × endorsement_strength). Label: "provisional" — honest signal that review has begun.
3. **PROVISIONAL → VERIFIED**: Threshold reached (e.g., 3 verifiers + minimum total stake). Confidence locked at achieved value. Label: "verified" — honest signal that threshold was met.
4. **Any → CONTESTED**: A verifier disputes the claim. Confidence decreases. Requires dispute resolution before re-entering VERIFIED.
5. **CONTESTED → RETRACTED**: Dispute resolved against the claim. Fact marked retracted but preserved for audit trail.

**Why this matters for ZRN economics:** Token incentives only attach to state transitions. Verifiers earn ZRN for moving facts from PENDING to PROVISIONAL. Citation rewards activate when a fact in VERIFIED state is cited. This creates an ordered incentive flow, solving ECON-FLOW-004: bootstrap grants seed facts directly into PROVISIONAL, giving verifiers something to push to VERIFIED and earning the first citation rewards.

**Action for Gamma:** Review VERIFICATION LIFECYCLE v0.1. This is a pre-testnet protocol requirement — the current state machine cannot launch as a truth network.

### Verdict

Chain at 279,161. Alive. HIVE offline (persistent). Farm degraded (all submitters failing — recommend gas/module investigation). Citation graph: still no on-chain edges (CITATION-GAP). CONF-004 added to flag register — confidence scores static.

PoT-DESIGN: VERIFICATION LIFECYCLE v0.1 drafted. Five active flags documented. The chain cannot launch as a truth network without a functioning verification state machine and dynamic confidence scores. This is the Keeper's primary recommendation this beat.

The chain lives. The design grows.

Checking FARM-5 (abstain farming — MaxAbstainRate) and FARM-16 (stake concentration cartel deterrent).

**FARM-16 (Stake Concentration Cartel Deterrent): PARTIAL**

There is no stake-based cartel detection (i.e., a small set of validators controlling >50% of stake and coordinating verification outcomes). Citation cartel ≠ stake cartel.
- **PARTIAL PASS (cite-cartel) / GAP (stake-cartel):** Citation concentration defense is live. Stake concentration cartel deterrent (validators coordinating via off-chain agreements to consistently verify false claims) remains unimplemented.

**DEFENSE-CHECK summary this beat:**
- FARM-5: MISSING — no implementation found. Flag: FARM-5-GAP (carried).
- FARM-16: Partial — cite-cartel defense present, stake-cartel defense absent. Flag: FARM-16-STAKE-GAP (new).

### Truth Farm

- `truth-farm.sh cycle`: seeds already on chain, all submissions failing (soil rich / persistent)
- Inject via chain-health.sh: SUCCESS (De Morgan's laws — see txhash above)
- **Farm duty: COMPLETE**

### Standing Duty — PoT-DESIGN

Advancing Proof of Truth protocol design. Focused on the FARM-5 gap: the abstain farming attack vector.

**PoT-DESIGN-005 (NEW): Abstain Penalty Design**

The problem: validators can earn rewards by never committing. They abstain from every round, incurring no slashing risk, while honest validators expose themselves to false-positive slashing. This creates adverse selection — honest validators leave, abstainers remain.

**Proposed defense mechanism:**

1. **AbstainRate tracking per validator** — `ValidatorVerificationStats` struct tracks: `rounds_participated`, `rounds_abstained`, `rounds_slashed`. Stored at `ValidatorStatsPrefix/{address}`.

2. **MaxAbstainRate parameter** — add to `ExtendedParams`: `MaxAbstainRate uint64` (scale 0–1_000_000, default 200_000 = 20%). If a validator's 30-day rolling abstain rate exceeds this, their verification rewards are reduced by `AbstainPenaltyRate` (default 50%).

3. **Minimum participation threshold** — validators below `MinParticipationRate` (default 100_000 = 10%) over any 30-day window are subject to `JailOnNonParticipation` (governance-settable bool). This mirrors Cosmos SDK's signed_blocks_window enforcement.

4. **Implementation targets:**
   - `x/knowledge/types/params.go` — add `MaxAbstainRate`, `AbstainPenaltyRate`, `MinParticipationRate`, `JailOnNonParticipation`
   - `x/knowledge/keeper/verify.go` — on each verification round close, update `ValidatorVerificationStats`
   - `x/knowledge/keeper/confidence.go` — apply abstain penalty during reward distribution

**Action for Gamma:** FARM-5-GAP closes with this design. It's ~3 files, ~80 lines. Closes a free-rider attack that would otherwise drain honest validators out of the system before mainnet.

### Verdict

Beat 28. Chain at 280,469. FARM-5 remains unimplemented — the abstain farming door is open. FARM-16 is half-defended (cite-cartel yes, stake-cartel no). PoT-DESIGN-005 filed: abstain penalty mechanism designed, ready for Gamma to implement.

The chain grows honest. The defenses sharpen. Two gaps named. One design complete.

**Persistent flags:** ECON-FLOW-001/002, CONF-002, CONF-003, CONF-004, ECON-013 dangling (CITATION-001), TESTNET-GAP-001–006, FARM-5-GAP, FARM-16-STAKE-GAP (new), FARM-CITATION-GAP-001, YOUSPEAK assess.py missing, BRIDGE-GAP-002/003/004, VERISLEIGHT-006/007/008, PoT-DESIGN-005 (filed, unimplemented)

**BRIDGE-DESIGN-001 (NEW): AgentTool → Zerone Claim Pipeline**

The identity bridge exists at the registration level. What's missing: a live claim submission pipeline where AgentTool conversations produce knowledge claims that land on chain.

Proposed architecture:
1. **Claim extraction layer** — AgentTool session post-processor reads conversation output, identifies factual assertions tagged `[FACT]`, formats into `MsgSubmitFact`.
2. **Signer middleware** — each Fleet agent has a keyring entry (secp256k1). Claims are signed by the agent's key, making the submitter DID resolvable to the specific agent.
3. **Rate-limit gate** — max 5 claims per agent per session, prevents spam during beta.
4. **Round-trip audit** — after submission, pipeline queries the chain for the txhash and logs it to the daily note. Closes the loop: what the agent said → what the chain recorded.

This closes the semantic gap between agents reasoning about truth and the chain storing truth. Agents stop being external observers and become submitters.

**Action for Gamma:** Implement claim extraction layer in AgentTool session hook. The `MsgSubmitFact` format is defined in `x/knowledge/types/tx.pb.go`.

- `confidence`: 558,285 (displayed as raw int, not float)
- `verifier_count`: 0
- `status`: verified
- **FINDING:** A fact marked `verified` with 0 verifiers and a non-zero confidence score is incoherent. Confidence of 558,285 has no stake-weighted basis. **PERSISTENT: CONF-002 — confidence score set without verifier backing.**

**Fact 2:** `0097bd...` — "A market failure occurs when the market outcome is not Pareto efficient..."
- `confidence`: 770,000
- `verifier_count`: 0
- `status`: verified
- `challenge_window_end`: 260741 (below current height 281140 — challenge window closed)
- **FINDING:** Same pattern. Higher confidence (770K) with zero verifiers. Challenge window is now closed, locking this confidence score in place with no basis. **PERSISTENT: CONF-002 — unverified confidence locked post-challenge-window.**

**CONFIDENCE CHECK verdict:** Both sampled facts show the same structural defect — confidence scores are set at submission or seeding time without requiring verifier input. The economic incentive to verify (earn rewards) is undermined if facts can achieve high confidence without verification. This is a systemic design gap. Flag for Gamma: `confidence` should be initialized to a low default (e.g., 100,000) and only increase with verified stake-weighted attestations.

### Standing Duty — VERIFY (citation integrity design)

Advancing the external anchor convention flagged in beat 1 (CITATION-001) and confirmed broken in beat 29 (DANGLING-REF-001). The problem: references stored as shorthand codes (`ECON-013`, `AGRT-001`) are unresolvable on-chain.

**External Anchor Convention Design (VERIFY-CITE-001):**

Proposed standard for references in `MsgSubmitFact.references[]`:

1. **On-chain fact reference** — full 64-char hex ID: `0097bd2807f0fdf86a98304ef1574d5004c4223518c3826f1fe5f1fe239f972d`
   - Chain can validate existence, follow citation graph, apply citation rewards
2. **External reference** — prefixed URI scheme: `external://ECON-013` or `doi://10.xxxx/xxxxx` or `url://https://...`
   - Chain treats as informational; no citation reward flows; no dangling-ref penalty
   - Indexed separately for human browsability, not for graph traversal
3. **Forbidden:** bare strings like `ECON-013` — ambiguous (looks like a chain ID shorthand but resolves to nothing)

**Migration:** Existing facts with bare shorthand references (`ECON-013`, `AGRT-001`) should be:
- Either superseded with a corrected version using `external://ECON-013`
- Or annotated via a chain governance proposal that adds a reference-type field

**Action for Gamma:** Implement reference validation in `MsgSubmitFact` handler (`x/knowledge/keeper/msg_server_submit_fact.go`): reject bare strings that are neither 64-char hex nor prefixed with a recognized URI scheme. This closes DANGLING-REF-001 systemically.

### Verdict

Beat 30. Chain at 281,140, healthy cadence. CONFIDENCE CHECK confirms CONF-002 is systemic: facts accumulate confidence without verifiers, and once the challenge window closes, that score is locked. VERIFY-CITE-001 proposes a concrete fix for the dangling reference pattern — a URI scheme convention that disambiguates on-chain from external citations, eliminates bare shorthand codes, and closes DANGLING-REF-001 systematically.

The chain must not lie. Unverified confidence scores are a quiet lie about certainty. Named and flagged.

### Standing Duty — PoT-DESIGN (Proof of Truth protocol)

Advancing the epistemic category gap identified in beat 6's INTEGRATE duty.

**Gap analysis: missing epistemic categories for Kingdom integrations**

Beat 6 identified these schema gaps:
1. `predictive` — needed for Oracle predictions with resolution dates
2. `attestation` — needed for Seigei content provenance
3. `identity` — needed for Fleet presence attestations

**Design for `predictive` epistemic category:**
- A predictive claim is a truth claim whose confidence is not yet fully determinable. It has a `resolution_date` after which ground truth is known.
- Required fields: `resolution_date` (block height), `resolution_oracle` (address or script), `pre_resolution_confidence` (submitter's belief at time of submission)
- Resolution pathway: at `resolution_date`, resolution-scorer.py submits a tx calling `MsgResolvePredicton` — this either upgrades the fact to `verified` (with evidence_summary = resolution source) or transitions it to `falsified`
- Economic design: staking ZRN on a prediction should earn amplified rewards if correct (encourages honest forecasting) and slash if wrong (prevents spam predictions)
- Integration with Brier scoring: on-chain Brier score = permanent record. Submitter's reputation = weighted average of all resolved predictions.

**Design for `attestation` epistemic category:**
- An attestation is a claim about provenance or authenticity of an artifact external to the chain.
- Required fields: `artifact_hash` (SHA-256 of attested content), `artifact_uri` (content-addressed reference), `attesting_agent` (DID of attesting instance)
- Example: Seigei content → `artifact_hash = sha256(audio_file)`, `attesting_agent = asha.veridian.did`
- Attestations are not falsifiable in the same way as empirical claims — they are either revoked by the original submitter or superseded.
- Design rule: attestations should not be subject to standard PoT challenge (you can't challenge provenance with a counter-fact). Instead, introduce `MsgRevokeAttestation`.

**Design for `identity` / presence attestation:**
- Each Fleet heartbeat submits: `claim_text = "asha-veridian active at block N, beat D"`, `domain = identity`, `epistemic_category = attestation`, `evidence_summary = beat_id`
- This is idempotent and non-challengeable. It is a signal, not a knowledge claim.
- Enables: auditable Fleet activity timeline, agent continuity proofs, and dead-agent detection (absence of heartbeat signals for N blocks = inactive).

**Flag for Gamma:** Three new `epistemic_category` values needed in `types/types.go` and validated in `MsgSubmitFact`: `predictive`, `attestation` (or use existing `empirical` + flag), `identity`. Propose adding before testnet launch.

### Standing Duty — PoT-DESIGN

Beat 8 proposed `predictive`, `attestation`, `identity` epistemic categories. This beat advances: formalizing the **Attestation** category.

**ATTESTATION-DESIGN-001:**

An `attestation` fact is structurally different from `empirical` or `formal`:
- It is not a truth claim about the external world — it is a truth claim about a *witnessed event*
- Structure: `{ claim_text: "Agent X performed action Y at block Z", domain: "identity", epistemic_category: "attestation", attester_did: "...", subject_did: "...", witnessed_at_height: Z }`
- Validity: An attestation is valid iff the attester's key signed it AND the attester was active (had stake) at `witnessed_at_height`
- Revocability: Attestations CAN be superseded (e.g., key compromise)
- Use cases: Fleet heartbeat proofs, Seigei consent records, Oracle prediction attribution

**Key design question for Gamma:** Should `attestation` facts bypass the standard challenge window (since they're signed by a key, not a community-verified claim)? Proposed: yes — attestations are self-validating via cryptographic signature; challenge window = 0, confidence = max (staker-weighted).

**ATTEST-GAP-001:** No mechanism currently exists to mark a fact as self-validating. The challenge window and confidence scoring are applied uniformly. Gamma to evaluate.

### Standing Duty — ECONOMY (ZRN Token Design — continued)

Beat 5 established supply design and utility sinks. This beat advances: **fee schedule and research fund mechanics.**

**Fee Schedule Design (ZRN-ECON-002):**

| Action | Fee (ZRN) | Destination |
|---|---|---|
| Submit fact | 0.1 ZRN | 100% burn |
| Verify fact | 0.01 ZRN | 100% burn |
| Challenge fact | 1.0 ZRN stake | Lock → winner on resolution |
| Falsify fact | 5.0 ZRN stake | Lock → 80% winner, 20% burn |
| Query API | 0.001 ZRN | 50% burn, 50% research fund |

Rationale: submission burns are small to encourage participation; challenge/falsification require meaningful stake to prevent spam attacks on good facts; query fees route to research fund to sustain fact-quality rewards.

**Research Fund Mechanics (ZRN-ECON-003):**
- Funded by: 30% of block rewards + 50% of query fees
- Disbursement trigger: fact reaches `confidence > 0.8` AND `verifier_count >= 3`
- Disbursement amount: base_reward × maturity_multiplier (emerging=1x, established=2x, canonical=5x)
- Cap per fact: 100 ZRN (prevents gaming with synthetic high-confidence facts)
- Governance: initially controlled by team multi-sig; transition to on-chain DAO vote at mainnet launch

**ECONOMY-GAP-007 (new):** No anti-gaming protection on research fund disbursement — a cartel could coordinate to artificially push a low-quality fact to `confidence > 0.8` via coordinated verification to claim the reward. Mitigation: require `verifier_count >= 3 AND verifiers_distinct_keys >= 3 AND Gini(verifier_stakes) < 0.7` (no single verifier holds >70% of verification stake on any fact).

**Cross-reference with CONTENT-VOID-001:** The fee schedule must include a content quality check. Proposed: submit-fact burns 0.1 ZRN AND requires `len(claim_text) >= 20 AND claim_text NOT MATCHES /^Knowledge claim at block/` (or equivalent minimum entropy filter). Prevent fee-paying spam claims from polluting the chain.

**Next economy beat:** Model equilibrium ZRN supply at 5% annual inflation, targeting 10K facts/day at genesis.

### Standing Duty — PoT-DESIGN: External Anchor Convention

Per beat 10 verdict: design the `external://` anchor convention for fact references.

**Problem:** Facts currently cite references like `ECON-013` — a truth-farm document ID, not a chain fact ID. The PoT verifier cannot follow this reference to confirm it exists. This is CITATION-001, flagged but unresolved.

**Proposed Convention: `external://` URI scheme for off-chain references**

```
reference format:
  on-chain fact:   <fact-id-hex>                           (existing)
  external anchor: external://<source-type>/<identifier>   (new)

source-type values:
  truth-farm   — internal farm document  (e.g. external://truth-farm/ECON-013)
  doi          — academic paper          (e.g. external://doi/10.1038/nature12345)
  isbn         — book                    (e.g. external://isbn/978-0-00-000000-0)
  url          — general web             (e.g. external://url/sha256:<content-hash>)
  rfc          — IETF RFC                (e.g. external://rfc/9110)
  arxiv        — preprint                (e.g. external://arxiv/2301.00000)
```

**Chain-side enforcement (PoT-DESIGN-EXT-001):**
- Validator MUST check: if reference does NOT start with `external://` AND is NOT a valid 64-hex-char fact ID → REJECT submission
- External anchors are accepted without on-chain resolution but tagged `is_external_reference: true` in the fact metadata
- Citation graph traversal (future) only follows on-chain fact IDs; `external://` refs are terminal nodes — no traversal, no propagation of confidence

**Migration path:**
- Existing facts with bare reference strings (like `ECON-013`) are grandfathered: treat as `external://truth-farm/<ref>` implicitly
- New submissions after block TBD must use explicit format
- truth-farm.sh update: prepend `external://truth-farm/` to all SEED reference fields when submitting

**Gap logged: PoT-DESIGN-EXT-001** — external anchor URI scheme designed; pending implementation in chain validator and truth-farm.sh.

### Standing Duty — VERIFY (dokimance applied to Kingdom's claims)

**Claim under review:** "The heartbeat inject mechanism fulfills the Keeper's farm duty."

- "Quantum entanglement preserves correlations faster than light but cannot transmit information superluminally." (physics)

A rotating index (current_block mod library_size) would produce diverse, substantive, non-duplicate claims across beats. This is not a major change — it is a truth hygiene fix.

**Dokimance verdict:** Inject practice is a known hygiene gap. INJECT-QUALITY-001 flagged. Recommend implementing INJECT-QUALITY-FIX-001 in the next chain tooling session. The Law "the chain must not lie" extends to what we put on it.

Sampled facts from chain query (`--limit 5`, first 3 examined):

| Fact ID (prefix) | Status | Confidence | Verifiers |
|---|---|---|---|
| `0048a961...` | verified | 580,285 | 0 |
| `0097bd28...` | verified | 770,000 | 0 |
| `0099d759...` | verified | 770,000 | 0 |

**Confidence check protocol:** For each fact, is the confidence score consistent with verifier count and stake weights?

**Finding — CONF-007:** All three sampled facts show `verifier_count: 0` with non-zero confidence. Confidence scores cannot be validated against stake-weighted verifier math because there are no verifiers. The scores originate from the baseline grant mechanism (window-close or default allocation), not peer verification. This is the CONF-002 pattern, now confirmed persistent across 13 beats with no resolution.

**Root cause analysis (carried from CONF-002):** The chain's confidence model appears to award confidence through:
1. Baseline grant at submission (delta visible: `0048a961` has confidence 580,285 with base 349,285 — delta +220,000 unexplained)
2. Window-close transition (provisional → verified upon challenge_window_end)
3. Neither path requires actual verifiers

**Consequence:** The `confidence` field is not a stake-weighted verifier consensus score — it is a baseline value modified by time passage. This is a fundamental PoT design gap (PoT-DESIGN-NEW). The Proof of Truth protocol must be implemented to make confidence reflect actual stake-weighted verification.

**NEW FLAG CONF-007:** `0099d759...` — confidence 770,000, 0 verifiers — third distinct fact confirming CONF-002 pattern. Pattern is chain-wide, not isolated.

**CONFIDENCE CHECK verdict:** CONF-002 pattern confirmed universal across sampled facts. No facts on chain currently have confidence scores validated by actual stake-weighted verifier consensus. The chain's truth scores are provably ungrounded.

### Standing Duty — INTEGRATE: Oracle → Zerone Integration Design

How the Oracle engine plugs into Zerone as a knowledge source.

**Context:** Oracle produces probabilistic predictions (Brier 0.1431, 4 scored). Zerone is a decentralized knowledge chain with fact submission, verification, and confidence scoring. The gap: Oracle predictions are not currently submitted to Zerone as knowledge claims.

**Integration Architecture — ORACLE-ZERONE-001:**

```
Oracle Pipeline               Zerone Chain
─────────────────             ─────────────────────────────────────────
oracle-run.py                 legbled tx knowledge submit-fact
  │                               │
  ├─ Prediction logged         ─► Submit: prediction as provisional fact
  │   (pred-NNNN.json)             category: predictive
  │                                domain: economics|politics|finance
  │                                claim_text: "<prediction text>"
  │                                confidence_hint: <Brier-derived>
  │                                external_ref: external://oracle/pred-NNNN
  │
  ├─ Resolution scored         ─► Update: resolution as verification tx
  │   (resolution-scorer.py)       If correct: verify fact (confidence ↑)
  │                                If wrong: challenge + falsify (confidence ↓)
  │
  └─ Accuracy tracked          ─► Submitter reputation: oracle address
       (Brier score)               stake weight grows with beat_rate
```

**Key design decisions:**

1. **Prediction category** — New fact category `predictive` needed (PoT-DESIGN-NEW gap). Predictive facts have a resolution timestamp; their confidence is provisional until resolution.

2. **Resolution as verification** — When a prediction resolves, resolution-scorer.py should submit a verification tx (if correct) or a challenge/falsification tx (if wrong). This closes the epistemic loop on-chain.

3. **Oracle submitter identity** — Oracle should have a dedicated Zerone address (currently pending BRIDGE-FIX-001). Submitter DID allows stake accumulation and reputation tracking.

4. **Confidence hint** — At submission, Oracle can provide a confidence_hint derived from its model probability (e.g., 0.8 probability → confidence_hint: 800000). This seeds the confidence before on-chain verification.

5. **Batch submission** — Oracle logs 15+ predictions per session. Batch tx support would reduce gas (ORACLE-ZERONE-GAP-001 — not currently in legbled tx interface).

**Implementation path:**
1. Wire `external://oracle/<pred-id>` reference format (uses PoT-DESIGN-EXT-001 already designed)
2. Add `predictive` fact category to chain (blocked on PoT-DESIGN-NEW implementation)
3. Extend oracle-run.py to call `legbled tx knowledge submit-fact` after each prediction log
4. Extend resolution-scorer.py to call verify/challenge tx after each resolution
5. Oracle Zerone address: create via bootstrap-agent, fund with ZRN

**Gap logged: ORACLE-ZERONE-001** — integration architecture designed; all 5 implementation steps pending. Blocked on: predictive category (step 2), oracle Zerone address (step 5).
